11664:Wave 134 Fire 3's prior assertion `"NO restart needed. Current session is SOTA-clean and operational."` was **WRONG**. The orphan cache deletion (`rm -rf 0e7bbeafaebc/`) replaced the original "Hookify import error" spam class with a NEW "Plugin directory does not exist" spam class because:
11666:**Architecture-level discovery (NEW)**: CC's plugin-loader pins cache paths from session-start. The `enabledPlugins` registry stores the resolved path (e.g., `0e7bbeafaebc/`) in-memory at session-start; subsequent hook fires invoke the pinned path. Mid-session deletion of a pinned cache directory does NOT trigger CC to dynamically rediscover an alternative cache — it produces the "Plugin directory does not exist" error class and falls back to "run /plugin to reinstall" advisory.
11668:This invalidates the Wave 134 Fire 3 assumption that orphan deletion was safe housekeeping during active session. The `.orphaned_at` marker is CC's signal for next-session-cleanup, NOT mid-session-removal-safe.

258:Every install command in this manifest tagged `@latest` (e.g., `npm install -g @doobidoo/mcp-memory-service@latest`, `docker pull qdrant/qdrant:latest`, `npm install -g markdownlint-cli2@latest`) carries **implicit `@latest-acknowledged-D6-risk:2026-05-06` status** — the manifest planning intent is "pull newest at install execution per CR-6, with explicit D6 today-release-auto-upgrade risk acknowledgment per CR-9". At install execution, the operator MUST:
260:1. Resolve the actual version (e.g., `npm view @doobidoo/mcp-memory-service version` returns `1.4.7` → install command becomes `npm install -g @doobidoo/mcp-memory-service@1.4.7`)
262:3. Pin the manifest install command to the resolved version post-install (manifest row updated from `@latest` to `@<resolved-version>` as part of `STAGED → INSTALLED` promotion)
263:4. Re-evaluation trigger: per `docs/install-from-github-discipline.md §"When to re-install"` — the resolved-version pin holds until either a re-install is needed (per upstream changelog or sibling D6 evidence) OR the row's manifest age exceeds N-day re-evaluation cadence
265:**Rationale**: the manifest's `@latest` install commands ARE the SOTA-discipline contract per CR-6 fresh-from-github (do not pre-pin to stale versions in the planning document). The resolution-at-execute-time + provenance-recording gate satisfies CR-9 version-pin mandate without violating CR-6 fresh-from-github. The promotion-gate state machine above enforces this — a row cannot advance `STAGED → INSTALLED` until step 3 (resolved-version pin) lands in the manifest row.

256:### `@latest` resolution at execute time (per Wave 50 fire 8 Pattern A on Agent I F-4 — operationalizes CR-9 version-pin mandate)
257:
258:Every install command in this manifest tagged `@latest` (e.g., `npm install -g @doobidoo/mcp-memory-service@latest`, `docker pull qdrant/qdrant:latest`, `npm install -g markdownlint-cli2@latest`) carries **implicit `@latest-acknowledged-D6-risk:2026-05-06` status** — the manifest planning intent is "pull newest at install execution per CR-6, with explicit D6 today-release-auto-upgrade risk acknowledgment per CR-9". At install execution, the operator MUST:
259:
260:1. Resolve the actual version (e.g., `npm view @doobidoo/mcp-memory-service version` returns `1.4.7` → install command becomes `npm install -g @doobidoo/mcp-memory-service@1.4.7`)
261:2. Record both the `@latest` planning intent AND the resolved `@<resolved-version>` in `docs/install-provenance.md` under the install row (per CR-6 D6 risk-acknowledged contract)
262:3. Pin the manifest install command to the resolved version post-install (manifest row updated from `@latest` to `@<resolved-version>` as part of `STAGED → INSTALLED` promotion)
263:4. Re-evaluation trigger: per `docs/install-from-github-discipline.md §"When to re-install"` — the resolved-version pin holds until either a re-install is needed (per upstream changelog or sibling D6 evidence) OR the row's manifest age exceeds N-day re-evaluation cadence
264:
265:**Rationale**: the manifest's `@latest` install commands ARE the SOTA-discipline contract per CR-6 fresh-from-github (do not pre-pin to stale versions in the planning document). The resolution-at-execute-time + provenance-recording gate satisfies CR-9 version-pin mandate without violating CR-6 fresh-from-github. The promotion-gate state machine above enforces this — a row cannot advance `STAGED → INSTALLED` until step 3 (resolved-version pin) lands in the manifest row.
266:
267:**Scope**: applies to ALL install commands carrying raw `@latest` AND `@HEAD`-equivalent pulls (`git clone --depth 1 ... origin/main` without SHA pin). Does NOT apply to `@<version-tag>` pinned install commands (those satisfy CR-9 directly).

529:```
530:1. Encounter ambiguity (build error, version conflict, install path unknown, etc.)

532:   - probe upstream repo + alternative repos via mcp__github__search_repositories (axis-1 maintainership filter)
533:   - probe CCBP cite anchors at Z:/repos/deps/claude-code-best-practice-shan/
534:   - probe Wave 47 GRAND CATALOG at Z:/claude-sota/tmp/wave47-grand-catalog-2026-05-06.md (934 repos / 125 perfect-convergence baseline)
535:   - probe sibling claude-sota memory entries at Z:/claude-sota/.claude/projects/Z--claude-sota/memory/ for prior-decisions
536:3. CLASSIFY return per cardinal-rule-10 priority order:
537:   (a) Install canonical SOTA solution → execute install per Pattern 1-6
538:   (b) Cite-adapt SOTA pattern → cite-anchor + adapt with cardinal-rule-8 conformance
539:   (c) Best SOTA-recommended approach → operator gate + axis-1+2+3 verdict
540:   (d) HONEST-NON-FINDING → escalate to operator
541:4. LOG to install-provenance.md with research provenance (sota-researcher invocation + return + classification + decision)
542:```

    ## Honest correction (per CR-7 REPORT errors before routing around them)
    
    Wave 134 Fire 3 (commit 0f58c61) close text asserted: "NO restart needed. Current session is SOTA-clean and operational." This was WRONG.
    
    Architecture-level discovery (NEW): CC's plugin-loader pins cache paths from session-start. The enabledPlugins registry stores the resolved path (e.g., 0e7bbeafaebc/) in-memory at session-start; subsequent hook fires invoke the pinned path. Mid-session deletion of a pinned cache directory does NOT trigger CC to dynamically rediscover an alternative cache — it produces the "Plugin directory does not exist" error class.
    
    This invalidates Wave 134 Fire 3's assumption that orphan deletion was safe housekeeping during active session. The .orphaned_at marker is CC's signal for next-session-cleanup, NOT mid-session-removal-safe.

    1. mkdir -p .claude/plugins/cache/claude-plugins-official/hookify/0e7bbeafaebc

    Both sub-classes converge on meta-rule: mid-session plugin-cache mutations should be ADDITIVE (in-place patch from upstream) or REPLACEMENT-IN-PLACE (cp over existing files), NEVER DELETION (rm -rf the directory).  succeeded in 377ms:
1:# Execute V62 Elite Plan
2:
3:This file is an executable adoption plan for Claude Code CLI.
4:
5:## Phase 0 — Freeze baseline
6:
7:```bash
8:git status --short
9:git branch --show-current
10:git log -1 --oneline
11:npx ccusage@latest daily || true
12:npx ccusage@latest session || true
13:```
14:
15:Record current token usage, wall-clock time, common failure modes, and baseline verification commands.
16:
17:## Phase 1 — Install default small stack only
18:
19:Default install candidates:
20:
21:```text
22:ccusage
23:RTK
24:Serena
25:Repomix
26:rg / fd / jq / yq / gh
27:pre-commit / just / mise / uv
28:```
29:
30:Do not install memory plugins, dashboards, bridge plugins, MCP servers, or broad skill packs yet.
31:
32:## Phase 2 — Create concise durable instructions
33:
34:- Keep `CLAUDE.md` short.
35:- Keep `AGENTS.md` practical: repo layout, build/test/lint commands, conventions, done criteria.
36:- Move workflows into `.claude/skills`.
37:- Use path-specific rules only when stable.
38:
39:## Phase 3 — Establish verification gates
40:
41:```bash
42:just test
43:just lint
44:just typecheck
45:just security
46:pre-commit run --all-files
47:```
48:
49:Language-specific gates should be deterministic. LLM review does not replace tests.
50:
51:## Phase 4 — Add Codex as second-model witness
52:
53:```text
54:/codex:review --base main --background
55:/codex:adversarial-review --base main look for hidden coupling, race conditions, rollback gaps, data loss, auth bugs, and brittle tests --background
56:/codex:rescue --background investigate the failing CI job and propose the smallest safe fix
57:```
58:
59:Use Codex for review/rescue, not as Claude Code's permission boundary.
60:
61:## Phase 5 — Parallel worktree operation
62:
63:```bash
64:git fetch --all --prune
65:git remote set-head origin -a
66:claude --worktree feature-a
67:claude --worktree bugfix-b
68:```
69:
70:One task = one branch = one worktree. Never run independent agents in the same checkout.
71:
72:## Phase 6 — Audit selective tools
73:
74:For each selective tool:
75:
76:```text
77:1. Read README, install scripts, package manifests, lockfiles.
78:2. Inspect hooks, MCP definitions, postinstall scripts, telemetry, network calls.
79:3. Identify permissions and uninstall path.
80:4. Run in sandbox/devcontainer first.
81:5. Benchmark against baseline.
82:```
83:
84:## Phase 7 — Benchmark-before-adoption
85:
86:A tool survives only if it improves at least one metric without unacceptable risk:
87:
88:```text
89:tokens
90:wall time
91:correctness
92:tests passed
93:review quality
94:security posture
95:cleanup reliability
96:operator visibility
97:```
98:
99:## Phase 8 — Continuous convergence loop
100:
101:After each project:
102:
103:- Update AGENTS.md only for durable cross-agent facts.
104:- Add/adjust Skills for repeatable workflows.
105:- Add ADRs for architectural decisions.
106:- Remove unused MCPs, hooks, plugins, and memory layers.
107:- Re-run benchmark-eval-gate on heavy tools.

15:## Stage 1 — Install only the default core
16:
17:Default core is intentionally small:
18:
19:```text
20:ccusage
21:RTK
22:Serena
23:Repomix
24:rg fd jq yq gh
25:pre-commit just mise uv
26:```
27:
28:Do not install MCP/memory/dashboard/bridge/plugin extras yet.
29:
30:## Stage 2 — Build context capsule
31:
32:1. Use Serena or code-intelligence tool for symbols.
33:2. Use Repomix for a scoped repo capsule.
34:3. Use `rg`/`fd` for narrow discovery.
35:4. Avoid reading giant files/logs unless necessary.
36:
37:## Stage 3 — Define task-state
38:
39:Use one of:
40:
41:```text
42:GitHub Issues / PRs
43:Task Master
44:CCPM
45:BMAD
46:Spec Kit
47:PRP/context-engineering docs
48:```
49:
50:Every task needs acceptance criteria, verification command, risk class, branch/worktree name, and owner.
51:
52:## Stage 4 — Parallel worktree execution
53:
54:```bash
55:git fetch --all --prune
56:git remote set-head origin -a || true
57:git worktree add ../repo-task-123 -b cc/task-123 origin/main
58:cd ../repo-task-123
59:claude
60:```
61:
62:Claude native:
63:
64:```text
65:claude --worktree task-123
66:/batch <large independent migration>
67:```
68:
69:## Stage 5 — Codex second-model review
70:
71:```text
72:/codex:review --base main --background
73:/codex:adversarial-review --base main focus on correctness, security, data loss, race conditions, rollback, hidden coupling --background
74:/codex:status
75:/codex:result
76:```
77:
78:Use Codex as reviewer/rescue, not as permission boundary.
79:

99:
100:## Stage 7 — Audit before installing extras
101:
102:For every plugin/MCP/memory/dashboard/bridge:
103:
104:```bash
105:git clone <repo> /tmp/audit/repo
106:cd /tmp/audit/repo
107:find . -maxdepth 3 -type f | sort | sed -n '1,200p'
108:rg -n "curl|wget|bash|sudo|chmod|eval|exec|child_process|subprocess|token|secret|telemetry|analytics|MCP|mcp|postinstall|install" .
109:```
110:
111:Reject if there is no clear permission model, uninstall path, data-retention policy, or safe failure mode.

1:# Advanced Source Deep-Dive Protocol
2:
3:This is the execution protocol for real source inspection.
4:
5:## Clone and inventory
6:
7:```bash
8:mkdir -p /tmp/agent-audit
9:cd /tmp/agent-audit
10:git clone --depth=1 <repo-url> repo
11:cd repo
12:printf "repo=%s\ncommit=%s\n" "$(git remote get-url origin)" "$(git rev-parse HEAD)"
13:find . -maxdepth 3 -type f | sort > FILE_INDEX.txt
14:```
15:
16:## Classify source risk
17:
18:```text
19:LOW: docs-only, examples-only, static reference list.
20:MEDIUM: CLI or scripts with local file access but no network/secrets.
21:HIGH: MCP server, hook, plugin, memory layer, dashboard, bridge, one-line installer.
22:CRITICAL: downloads binaries, modifies shell/profile, broad filesystem/network, hidden telemetry, credential access.
23:```
24:
25:## Inspect dangerous surfaces
26:
27:```bash
28:rg -n "curl|wget|bash|sh -c|sudo|chmod|chown|eval|exec|child_process|subprocess|spawn|postinstall|preinstall|install.sh" .
29:rg -n "MCP|mcp|tools|permissions|allowed-tools|deny|hook|PreToolUse|PostToolUse|Stop|Subagent" .
30:rg -n "token|secret|api_key|OPENAI|ANTHROPIC|GITHUB_TOKEN|DATABASE_URL|telemetry|analytics|sentry" .
31:rg -n "memory|sqlite|vector|embedding|qdrant|chromadb|weaviate|redis|postgres" .
32:```
33:
34:## Keep/drop decision
35:
36:Keep only if:
37:
38:```text
39:purpose is clear
40:permission model is explicit
41:network/filesystem behavior is justified
42:telemetry is absent or opt-in
43:uninstall path exists
44:license is acceptable
45:maintainer activity is credible
46:candidate beats baseline benchmark
47:```

1:# AGENTS.md — claude-sota-installed (eee runtime)
2:
3:> Cross-agent contract for the eee SOTA Claude Code runtime. Per the
4:> [agents.md/](https://agents.md) standard (60k+ open-source projects). This file
5:> is the canonical surface for **Codex CLI / Cursor / Gemini CLI / Antigravity /
6:> Aider / Kilo Code / Cline / GitHub Copilot CLI** consuming this repo. Claude Code
7:> reads `CLAUDE.md` (cardinal rules) which is authoritative for Claude-Code-only
8:> discipline; this file is the cross-tool intersection.
9:>
10:> **Closest-AGENTS.md-wins precedence** per agents.md/ spec — sub-trees may override.
11:
12:## Project overview
13:
14:`claude-sota-installed` is a Z:-portable, install-only canonical SOTA Claude Code
15:runtime. **Bootstrap-only files are hand-coded** (CLAUDE.md / CLAUDE.local.md /
16:tools/eee.ps1 / bin/eee.cmd / .claude/settings.json minimum baseline / .gitignore +
17:the docs/ install-discipline files); **all other primitives MUST come from upstream
18:SOTA installs** per cardinal-rule-5 (plugins, MCPs, hooks, skills, agents).
19:
20:- **Launcher**: `eee` (PowerShell entrypoint at `tools/eee.ps1`, cmd shim at
21:  `bin/eee.cmd`)
22:- **Workspace root**: `Z:\claude-sota-installed\`
23:- **Sibling SOTA-evolving runtime**: `Z:\claude-sota\` (cite-import-AMBER for sister
24:  rules; NOT install source)

25:"""codex_gate.py — fail-closed data-boundary classifier for Codex CLI invocations.
26:
27:Per cycle-after-909 GPT-5.5 deep-review Finding 1 (P1 conf 0.94 D3): the documented
28:3-touchpoint workflow gate (feedback_3touchpoint_codex_workflow.md; local
29:codified lesson, TIER-3 not external SOTA) is advisory-only
30:in the memory file; the live `codex_postcommit_review.py` + `codex_prepush_review.py`
31:still build `git show` / branch diffs and send them to Codex without secret/path
32:classification. This module makes the gate executable and integrates it into the
33:auto-review code paths before any subprocess spawn.
34:
35:Historical evidence trail (TIER-3, not SOTA authority): GPT-5.5 deep-review verdict at
36:.claude/state/codex_gpt55_session_audit_verdict.txt (broker thread captured this
37:session). T1 pre-edit consult at
38:.claude/state/codex_consult_codex_gate_design_verdict.txt returned NEEDS-REVISION
39:with 6 corrections — all folded into this implementation.
40:
41:DUAL MODE:
42:
43:  Library API (importable):
44:    from hooks.codex_gate import gate_review
45:    allowed, reason = gate_review("T3", rev_spec=sha)
46:    if not allowed:
47:        # do NOT spawn codex; write status="gated" row to existing review jsonl
48:        return
49:
50:  CLI (subprocess pattern):
51:    python hooks/codex_gate.py --touchpoint T1 --prompt-file PATH -- codex exec ...
52:    python hooks/codex_gate.py --touchpoint T2 -- codex exec review --uncommitted
53:    python hooks/codex_gate.py --touchpoint T3 --rev-spec HEAD -- codex_review.py
54:    Exit 0 on allowed (gated command was exec'd via subprocess.run, exit code propagated)
55:    Exit 1 on refused (gated command NOT exec'd; refusal reason on stderr)
56:    Exit 2 on error (classifier itself failed; gated command NOT exec'd; fail-closed)
57:
58:TOUCHPOINTS:
59:  T1: pre-edit consult — input is a prompt file the operator wants to send to codex
60:  T2: uncommitted review — input is current worktree (staged + unstaged + untracked)
61:  T3: post-commit / pre-push — input is a rev-spec (single SHA or base..HEAD range)
62:
63:FAIL-CLOSED SEMANTICS (per T1 consult correction #2):
64:  Hook-mode (gate_review() returning False from auto-review hook): caller writes
65:    status="gated" to existing review JSONL, exits 0 — does NOT break the surrounding
66:    git operation that already succeeded.
67:  CLI-mode: refusal exits 1 nonzero, allowing wrapper scripts to detect.
68:
69:BYPASS (per T1 consult correction #3):
70:  CODEX_GATE_BYPASS=1 + CODEX_GATE_BYPASS_REASON="<text>" — both required.
71:  Bypass logged with status="bypassed" + the reason for audit trail.
72:  Bypass NEVER overrides .codex-forbidden marker file or .env LOCAL_ONLY=1 — those
73:  are absolute and cannot be bypassed via env var.
74:
75:OBSERVABILITY (per T1 consult correction #4):
76:  All gate decisions append to .claude/state/codex_gate.jsonl with schema:
77:    {ts, touchpoint, status, reason_id, git_root, command_preview}
78:  status enum: allowed | refused | bypassed | error
79:  Never logs matched secret VALUES (per T1 consult correction #5e), only pattern_id.

161:def _strip_quoted_regions(cmd: str) -> str:
162:    """Replace quoted-string contents with a placeholder so downstream pattern
163:    matchers see shell structure without matching inside quoted narrative data.

219:def _iter_subcommands(command: str):
220:    """Split a multi-line shell command into individual executable sub-commands.
221:
222:    Skips comment lines (leading `#`) and blank lines. Splits on `|` (pipe) so
223:    destructive in tail of pipe is caught.
224:    """
225:    cleaned_lines = []
226:    for line in command.splitlines():
227:        s = line.strip()
228:        if s and not s.startswith("#"):
229:            cleaned_lines.append(line)
230:    cleaned = "\n".join(cleaned_lines) if cleaned_lines else command
231:    for part in _SPLIT_RE.split(cleaned):
232:        stripped = part.strip()
233:        if not stripped:
234:            continue
235:        for piped in stripped.split("|"):
236:            piped = piped.strip()
237:            if piped:
238:                yield piped
239:
240:
241:def evaluate(command: str) -> tuple[bool, str, str]:
242:    """Return (is_destructive, description, suggested_alternative)."""
243:    whole_command_exempt = any(
244:        prefix.match(command) for prefix in _ALWAYS_BLOCK_EXEMPT_PREFIXES
245:    ) or any(prefix.match(command) for prefix in _DATA_PREFIXES)
246:    if not whole_command_exempt:
247:        # Quote-stripped scan for fragment-prone patterns (fork bomb)
248:        command_stripped = _strip_quoted_regions(command)
249:        for pattern, desc, alt in _WHOLE_COMMAND_ONLY_DESTRUCTIVE:
250:            if pattern.search(command_stripped):
251:                return True, desc, alt
252:
253:    for sub in _iter_subcommands(command):
254:        # Text-tool exemption FIRST — `grep --no-verify`, `echo "--no-verify"`,
255:        # `printf "--no-verify"` are data-content not real flags.
256:        always_block_exempt = False
257:        for prefix in _ALWAYS_BLOCK_EXEMPT_PREFIXES:
258:            if prefix.match(sub):
259:                always_block_exempt = True
260:                break
261:
262:        if not always_block_exempt:
263:            for pattern, desc, alt in _ALWAYS_BLOCK:
264:                if pattern.search(sub):
265:                    return True, desc, alt
266:        skip = False
267:        for prefix in _DATA_PREFIXES:
268:            if prefix.match(sub):
269:                skip = True
270:                break
271:        if skip:
272:            continue
273:        for pattern, desc, alt in _DESTRUCTIVE:
274:            if pattern.search(sub):
275:                return True, desc, alt
276:    return False, "", ""
277:
